## `AFG-6201-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** JMeter-APM-Bottleneck Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Code Coverage Unit Testing & High-Concurrency Performance Stress
- **Intro:** JMeter-APM-Bottleneck Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Code Coverage Unit Testing & High-Concurrency Performance Stress — Pre-Calibration System State Verification: CPU usage must be below 70%, memory usage below 80%, disk I/O below 60%, and network latency below 10 ms.
